On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1k, CIRCLE is competitive at 10-20 task splits and substantially outperforms strong CS-EFCIL baselines at 50, 100, and 500 task splits, while training much faster than trained-backbone drift-compensation methods. Ablations show that the BiRC2D-style extractor, SLDA head, and balanced feature/prediction ensembling each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [146] [Transformer-Based Classification of Bacterial Raman Spectra with LOOCV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27096)
*Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于Transformer的模型在拉曼光谱分类中的表现，发现其优于传统机器学习方法，且在无预处理情况下仍保持优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类领域受到越来越多关注，但需要系统评估其相对于传统机器学习方法的性能优势，并验证其在实际应用中的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用嵌套留一重复交叉验证框架，将基于Transformer的方法与结合PCA或ICA的传统机器学习流程（LDA、SVM、随机森林分类器）进行系统比较。使用包含6种细菌、9个独立测量重复的5,417个单细胞光谱的细菌拉曼数据集进行评估。

Result: Transformer在独立测试重复中始终保持最高分类性能，显著优于所有传统方法。潜在特征空间分析显示其类别分离度优于PCA和ICA表示。Transformer直接应用于原始拉曼光谱（无预处理）时仍保持优越性能，在测量重复间表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的模型在鲁棒拉曼光谱分类方面具有巨大潜力，强调了重复感知验证对于实际模型评估的重要性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently attracted increasing attention for Raman spectral classification. In this study, a transformer-based approach was systematically evaluated using a nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation framework and compared with conventional machine-learning pipelines combining PCA or ICA with LDA, SVM, and Random Forest classifiers. A bacterial Raman dataset comprising 5,417 single-cell spectra from six bacterial species and nine independent measurement replicates was used. The transformer consistently achieved the highest classification performance across independent test replicates and significantly outperformed all conventional approaches. Analysis of the learned latent feature space revealed improved class separation compared with PCA- and ICA-based representations. Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [147] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了修正的Heavy-ball Q-learning方法并证明其收敛性，在特定条件下可实现比标准Q-learning更快的收敛速度，并通过切换线性系统(SLS)视角提供了新的理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Q-learning算法的收敛速度可能较慢，而Heavy-ball动量方法在优化领域已被证明可以加速收敛。然而，现有Q-learning分析中缺乏对动量加速机制的深入理论理解，特别是缺乏保证收敛和加速的理论条件。这促使作者建立一个新的理论框架来分析和加速Q-learning算法。

Method: 作者采用切换线性系统(SLS)表示来建模Q-learning算法，并利用相关切换族的联合谱半径(JSR)进行分析。首先提出修正的Heavy-ball Q-learning方法，然后建立其SLS表示，通过JSR分析推导收敛条件和加速条件。同样的构造方法被扩展到线性函数逼近的Q-learning中。

Result: 证明了修正Heavy-ball Q-learning方法的收敛性，确定了其比标准Q-learning收敛更快的理论条件。在线性函数逼近场景下，获得了类似的收敛性保证和加速结论。SLS框架为理解Heavy-ball动量如何加速Q-learning提供了新的理论视角。

Conclusion: 基于切换线性系统表示的分析框架为Q-learning算法研究提供了补充性的理论基础，不仅证明了Heavy-ball动量在Q-learning中的有效性，还建立了保证收敛和加速的明确条件，为设计更高效的强化学习算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [148] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Uplift建模中个体处理效应估计面临的挑战，提出了CHAUN模型和RA-IPS方法，在公开数据集和生产数据集上取得了显著性能提升，有效解决了组间相似性利用和未观测混杂因素的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uplift建模在估计个体处理效应(ITE)时面临两大核心挑战：一是如何灵活利用组间相似性以增强判别能力，二是在存在未观测混杂因素场景下的去偏差问题。现有方法在这些方面存在局限性，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 本文提出两个核心方法：(1)Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN)：采用共享特征嵌入和跨头注意力机制，动态整合处理组和对照组的特定表示，增强组间相关性建模；(2)Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS)：在约束不确定性集合内对抗性优化倾向性权重，缓解未观测变量带来的偏差。理论上证明了在获得真实倾向性分数的情况下，即使存在未观测混杂因素也能保证ITE的可识别性。

Result: 在公开数据集(CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA)和生产电商数据集上的实验表明：CHAUN相比最先进的Uplift模型在QINI分数上实现了最高25.6%的相对提升；RA-IPS在未观测混杂情况下相比标准IPS方法提升了5.4%的性能。结果验证了所提方法在实际因果推断任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CHAUN模型和RA-IPS方法有效解决了Uplift建模中的关键挑战，在理论和实践层面都取得了重要突破。CHAUN通过注意力机制增强了组间相似性建模，RA-IPS提升了在未观测混杂场景下的鲁棒性，为实际因果推断应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [149] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN在流体动力学代理建模任务（预测翼型表面压力分布）中的表现，发现其性能略逊于MLP且远不如GNN，尽管模型复杂度更低且训练更快，但存在训练不稳定和超参数敏感等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN作为一种新型神经网络架构，通过自适应激活函数而非传统MLP中的仿射变换系数来构建模型。尽管KAN具有通用近似性质且引起了广泛关注，但其在符号回归、机器学习、自然语言处理和计算机视觉等领域的优越性仍存在争议。本文旨在评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模领域相对于MLP和GNN的实际表现。

Method: 选择翼型表面压力分布预测作为气动动力学中的典型任务，在亚音速和跨音速条件下，对比评估KAN、MLP和GNN三种架构的性能。评估指标包括压力系数预测精度、跨马赫数和攻角的插值能力、模型复杂度、训练时间以及训练稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明：KAN能够较好地预测整体压力系数分布，并具备跨马赫数和攻角的插值能力；然而其性能与经过适当训练的MLP相当（略微逊色），而GNN在更长的训练时间代价下取得了最优性能；KAN模型复杂度通常远低于MLP和GNN，从而实现更快的训练速度，但存在训练不稳定问题，且性能高度依赖于超参数优化的质量。

Conclusion: KAN在流体动力学代理建模中展现出一定的潜力，具有模型复杂度低和训练速度快的优势，但在预测精度上尚未超越传统架构。KAN面临的主要挑战是训练不稳定性和超参数敏感性，这限制了其在当前阶段的实际应用优势。未来研究需要解决这些训练问题以充分发挥KAN的潜力。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [150] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了fTNN方法，一种确定性张量神经网络子空间方法，用于解决有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯算子问题，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程在物理建模中日益重要，但分数阶拉普拉斯算子的非局部性带来了数值计算挑战。现有方法如蒙特卡洛积分存在计算效率低和精度不足的问题，特别是在处理低正则性解和强边界奇异性时表现不佳。因此需要开发高精度、确定性的数值方法来有效求解此类问题。

Method: 提出fTNN确定性张量神经网络子空间方法：(1)设计几何自适应积分分裂方案，采用空间相关的近场半径将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内部远场和解析外部远场三部分；(2)对奇异径向积分采用Gauss-Jacobi求积，规则径向积分采用Gauss求积，角度变量采用确定性角度求积，构建全确定性积分框架；(3)构建边界奇异性感知的试探函数，融入显式边界特征，并提出自动选择主导指数和评估损失函数的策略；(4)针对时间相关问题设计时空可分离神经网络，结合交替神经网络子空间优化策略进行高效训练。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在所有测试基准问题上均达到高精度，相比现有的fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法有显著改进。特别是在强边界奇异性问题和长时间模拟问题上，该方法展现出明显优势，验证了确定性积分框架和边界奇异性感知策略的有效性。

Conclusion: fTNN方法成功构建了分数阶拉普拉斯算子的全确定性积分框架，通过几何自适应积分分裂和边界奇异性感知策略，有效解决了分数阶偏微分方程求解中的关键难点。该方法为分数阶偏微分方程的高精度数值求解提供了新的有效途径，在复杂边界条件和长时间演化问题上具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [151] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型辅助采样框架，通过融合抽样调查理论来降低随机梯度估计的方差，在多种基准数据集上实现了显著的性能提升，特别是在与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，仅需一半训练轮数即可获得更好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习依赖于小批量随机梯度下降等优化方法，这些方法虽然能够近似全梯度但会引入噪声，在收敛稳定性、速度和泛化能力之间产生权衡。现有的方差减少技术（如SVRG和SAG）和自适应优化器虽然旨在缓解梯度噪声，但可能带来额外的计算开销。

Method: 作者提出了模型辅助采样框架，将小批量梯度估计问题纳入抽样调查理论框架中，将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度作为样本估计。通过引入辅助梯度预测模型，构建更高效的梯度估计器。当不使用辅助信息时，该方法退化为均匀抽样。该框架可与现有优化器无缝集成，在不改变其动态特性的前提下提高效率。

Result: 在合成数据和六个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，在71-86%的实验中获得了性能提升，特别是在中等规模输入空间的基准测试中表现突出。值得注意的是，在与基于动量的优化器（如AdamW）配合使用时，所提估计器仅需约一半的训练轮数即可实现明显更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将机器学习优化与抽样调查理论相结合，为随机梯度估计问题提供了新的理论视角和实用解决方案。模型辅助采样框架不仅具有理论价值，还在实际应用中展现出显著的效率和泛化性能优势，为优化算法的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [152] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了RecallRisk-BERT多任务学习框架，用于自动化FDA医疗器械召回记录的严重程度分级和根因分类，在单任务和多任务设置中均取得了优异性能（准确率0.963，ROC-AUC 0.974），为召回分类和风险评估提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDA医疗器械召回记录快速增长，给召回分类、严重程度评估和根因解释带来挑战。现有研究大多分别处理召回预测或根因分析，而对召回严重程度和根因类别的联合建模研究有限，缺乏自动化召回分类的综合框架。

Method: 研究基于2002年至2025年10月的54,165条FDA医疗器械召回记录，首先评估经典机器学习和boosting模型，然后开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型。该模型结合PubMedBERT的召回叙述文本表示与产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化类别特征的嵌入表示，同时预测召回严重程度（I/II/III级）和9类根因类别。

Result: 在单任务严重程度预测中，基于LightGBM的文本-表格配置表现最佳，准确率0.963，宏F1值0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974。在多任务设置中，RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线。模型推导的风险排序与观测到的根因严重程度模式高度一致（rho=0.983，p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格学习方法能够支持可扩展的召回后分类、监管决策支持和基于模型的根因风险分析，为医疗器械召回管理提供了有效的自动化工具。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [153] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型(VLM)学习基于势能的奖励塑形(PBRS)中的势能函数，有效解决了稀疏奖励强化学习中的探索困难问题，在保证最优策略不变的前提下显著提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励环境中的强化学习面临两大挑战：(1)缺乏中间反馈信号指导探索；(2)难以将稀疏的成功奖励正确归因到轨迹的相关部分。传统的奖励塑形方法容易导致奖励黑客(reward hacking)问题，而基于势能的奖励塑形(PBRS)虽然能保证最优策略集不变，但需要人工设计启发式势能函数。因此，如何自动学习有效的势能函数成为关键问题。

Method: 提出VLM-PBRS框架，通过以下方式学习势能函数：(1)使用轻量级视觉语言模型(VLM)对图像对进行偏好标注；(2)利用这些偏好标签训练势能函数模型；(3)将学习到的势能函数应用于PBRS框架中。该方法采用小型、计算效率更高的VLM以降低计算成本，尽管其偏好标签准确性较低，但实证表明仍能有效加速学习。由于基于PBRS理论，该方法天然保持原始最优策略集不变。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中进行了广泛实验验证，证明了：(1)该方法能够显著提升样本效率；(2)对奖励黑客具有鲁棒性；(3)VLM偏好标签准确性与样本效率提升之间存在关联性；即使使用准确性较低的小型VLM，仍能有效加速学习过程。

Conclusion: 本文做出了三项主要贡献：(1)首次将VLM偏好学习应用于合成PBRS的势能函数；(2)提出了一种原则性、低成本的解决方案，利用小型VLM实现高效学习；(3)通过大量实验证明了方法在样本效率提升和抗奖励黑客方面的有效性。该框架为稀疏奖励强化学习提供了一种自动化、高效的奖励塑形解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [154] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架的新型归因方法，通过分析事件相关变量的完整信息流来提升事件时序图神经网络（ETGNNs）的可解释性，在合成数据集和真实数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅关注信息流的一部分（通常仅追踪事件相关嵌入到输出的贡献），忽略了通过事件诱导变量传递信息的重要路径，而这些变量在节点间交互和捕获长期时序依赖中起核心作用。

Method: 基于归一化相关性度量（NRM）框架，分析所有事件相关变量的完整信息流，包括源于事件嵌入的信息流和通过事件诱导变量传递的信息流；扩展NRM框架并提出模块化分解过程，以系统性地构建复杂神经架构的相关性结构，确保各层潜在变量的可比性并支持事件间交互的高阶分析。

Result: 在流行病追踪和社会动态的合成数据集以及政治事件网络的现实数据集上，定性和定量实验均表明该方法持续优于现有解释方法，并能生成更易于人类理解的解释。

Conclusion: 所提出的归因方法通过分析完整的ETGNN信息流并考虑事件诱导变量的关键作用，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，在多个应用场景中实现了更优的解释性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [155] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统性地构建了图神经网络（GNN）的统一设计空间，从第一性原理推导了谱方法和空间方法，并将其表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次结构关联。文章深入分析了十二个应用领域，揭示了异配性、规模、时间动态性等跨领域共性挑战，并指出过度平滑、过度压缩、鲁棒性等关键问题是决定GNN能否实际部署的核心约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络已从边缘技术成为处理关系数据的主流模型。当前的核心问题不再是消息传递是否有效，而是明确在何种场景下图结构的计算成本是合理的。因此，需要建立一个统一的设计空间和方法论框架，系统评估GNN的理论表达能力和实际应用效果之间的差距。

Method: 该综述围绕单一设计空间组织领域知识，从共享的第一性原理推导谱公式和空间公式，并将模型表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次建立联系，明确指出当前架构能分离和不能分离的结构。在此基础上，对推荐系统、社交网络、知识图谱、药物发现、医疗健康、计算机视觉等十二个应用领域进行详细审查，分析每个领域的图构建选择、主导架构及其原因。

Result: 跨领域比较揭示了若干重复模式：异配性（heterophily）和大规模几乎在所有领域都对相同模型构成挑战；时间图比静态图更难处理；在公共排行榜上表现最好的架构往往不是实际部署中使用的架构。此外，过度平滑、过度压缩、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性等问题是决定模型是否被采纳的关键约束。研究还区分了真实增益与弱基线或有偏数据划分带来的虚假改进。

Conclusion: 图神经网络领域需要从单纯追求基准测试性能转向解决实际部署约束。过度平滑、过度压缩、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性和可解释性不应被视为结束时的检查清单，而是决定技术能否被采纳的核心约束。未来的研究应更关注这些实际问题，以及异配性、大规模和时间动态性等跨领域的共性挑战。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [156] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FisherSketch方法，通过单次流式传递估计头Fisher对齐，解决了共享词表LLM家族在科学序列领域（如SMILES、蛋白质和基因组序列）的训练无关源选择问题。该方法仅需16KB任务签名，能在表示相似性指标失效时仍有效区分任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享词表的科学序列领域（SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列），候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同，形成了"激活-暗"机制：表示相似性指标在不假设标签条件误差几何的情况下无法提供信息，而经典更新几何指标在词表规模下计算代价过高。此外，在共享输出头设置中，表示指标（如CKA）对于迁移是不可识别的——模型可能共享相同表示却具有正交的头更新。

Method: 提出FisherSketch方法，核心思想是头Fisher对齐恰好等于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入之间的余弦值，从而暴露激活、误差和耦合因子，而无需显式的Fisher矩阵。该方法直接在单次流式传递中估计该余弦值，仅需16KB任务签名（m=4096）和192KB每任务流状态，小到可以存储在模型哈希旁边，但编码了迁移相关的更新结构。

Result: 使得K=128,256头Fisher对齐在实践中可行；通过共享参数和内层验证，以及Llama-3.1-8B语言器移位实验，证明FisherSketch在激活相似性无法区分任务时仍能保持信息性；相同的签名和边际分布提供了诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是其耦合驱动。

Conclusion: FisherSketch成功解决了共享词表LLM的源选择问题，突破了表示相似性指标的不可识别性限制；该方法不仅具有实用价值（低存储开销、单次流式计算），还为理解LLM任务相似性的驱动因素提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [157] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可解的高维GAN训练模型，发现低秩相关性能提升弱方向的 learnability，而强相关性会破坏恢复过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解GAN分析假设信号无条件且具有对角潜在协方差，本文旨在将多特征判别器设置扩展到类依赖、相关和非零均值的潜在结构，以研究更复杂的数据结构对GAN训练动力学的影响。

Method: 建立了一个可解的高维GAN训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。采用二次能量判别器，证明随机微观训练过程在高维极限下收敛为由有效协方差控制的确定性常微分方程（ODE）。在匹配协方差特化中进行稳定性分析。

Result: 稳定性分析揭示了一个由学习率和噪声水平决定的模态可解区间：学习始于主导有效特征值穿过下阈值，完全恢复要求所有相关有效模态保持在区间内。发现了一种信号增强机制：低秩相关性可以将弱方向提升到可学习阈值之上，而过度强的相关性会破坏恢复过程。数值模拟验证了ODE、相边界和增强机制。

Conclusion: 在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，信息化的生成器协方差改善了与数据驱动的参考子空间的对齐。该工作为理解GAN训练中数据结构的作用提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [158] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了关系压力与精神病学语料库(RSPC)，通过Reddit异地恋帖子数据，研究表明结合人际背景能提升心理健康建模效果，不同模型在各任务上表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言处理研究通常将心理健康状况作为孤立现象建模，缺乏人际背景语境。作者旨在捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的关系触发因素，推动从以个人为中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。

Method: 构建包含1,799篇Reddit帖子的关系压力与精神病学语料库(RSPC)，由精神科医生标注诊断类别（包括焦虑和抑郁等情绪障碍）、关系压力触发因素以及关系阶段指示。在多标签障碍分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三个任务上，对七个微调的Transformer模型和五个大语言模型进行基准测试。

Result: 不同模型家族在任务间存在明显差异：Claude-3-Haiku在障碍分类任务上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测任务上获得最强性能（Macro-F1=0.519）。研究还发现焦虑障碍与长期关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，支持心理健康建模从以个人为中心转向情境感知的方法，能够捕捉困扰的社会和时间动态特征。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [159] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个社交媒体博彩广告数据集，包含操纵性和欺骗性标注及人工解释，支持可解释性检测研究，并分析了对用户心理健康的潜在影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上博彩应用的推广广告日益增多，这些广告使用劝诱技术可能误导用户、鼓励冒险行为并影响心理健康。然而，由于缺乏公开的标注数据集，针对操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的自动检测研究仍然有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个广泛使用的社交媒体平台收集博彩相关广告，进行人工标注以识别操纵性和欺骗性广告行为。除分类标签外，数据集还包括人工提供的解释，描述每个标注背后的推理过程，从而支持可解释性检测方法的研究。

Result: 构建了一个包含标注和解释的博彩广告数据集，分析了博彩广告中常用的策略，并研究了这些劝诱策略如何可能影响用户的心理健康。提出的框架可实现浏览器插件警告用户和自动化网络爬虫帮助监管机构监控检测等功能。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了博彩广告自动检测领域的数据空白，为开发可解释性检测方法提供了基础，有助于保护用户免受操纵性广告影响，并为监管机构提供技术支持以在线监控此类推广活动。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [160] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了时间序列预测中"模型越大越好"的假设，通过优化预处理策略而非扩大模型规模，以低成本实现了高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测研究普遍认为更大的模型架构是实现高精度的关键，作者反对这一观点，提出通过优化预处理而非扩大模型规模，能够以更低的成本缩小性能差距。

Method: 以Ridge回归为测试平台（因其具有闭式解和可解释权重），在八个标准基准数据集上对上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强等预处理超参数进行系统搜索，分析最优超参数的模式与数据结构的关系。

Result: 发现三个关键模式：（1）最优回溯窗口长度具有强序列特异性，与预测视野呈非单调关系，挑战了长视野需要长历史的传统假设；（2）在上下文的学习尾部分数上进行归一化优于全上下文归一化；（3）同数据集内不同序列的最优超参数往往不同，最优跨序列共享程度因数据集而异。优化后的模型在多数数据集-视野组合上超越先前线性预测器，并在六个基准上优于Transformer、MLP和CNN基线。

Conclusion: 通过优化预处理而非扩大模型规模，能够以低成本实现竞争性预测性能；优化后的超参数可作为数据本身的诊断工具，揭示大型模型通过学习参数静默吸收的数据结构特征。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [161] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了ODE的可识别性界限，并分析了从解数据学习控制方程的样本复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测解数据学习控制方程是科学机器学习中的基本挑战，然而关于从多个解观测中唯一且稳定识别真实ODE的理论条件尚不完善，文献中也缺乏对此类学习任务样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，该度量捕捉了两个方程在所有允许初始条件下的最坏情况分离，从而编码了识别问题的极小极大结构。基于此度量推导了相关ODE类的度量熵估计，并分析样本复杂度界限。

Result: 在广泛的方程类（从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz（Hölder）连续向量场的非线性类）中建立了控制ODE的可识别性界限，精确刻画了何时可以从解数据区分两个不同的方程；量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的解观测数量。

Conclusion: 该工作为从解数据学习控制ODE提供了理论保障，包括可识别性条件和样本复杂度界限，为科学机器学习中的方程发现任务建立了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [162] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用RLAIF框架生成可迁移的求职查询，发现奖励塑造比优化器选择更关键，并提出基于规则的奖励下限来缓解GRPO算法的奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职平台的低带宽查询接口无法捕捉候选人简历的高维复杂性，需要生成可迁移的求职查询——即抽象掉特定求职者标识符但保留可泛化资质的术语。然而，该任务存在高度对抗性的奖励表面，策略优化常利用LLM评估标准的缺陷，导致退化的逐字复制行为。

Method: 提出了端到端的RLAIF框架，进行了全面的实证实验以隔离优化机制与结构化奖励工程的影响。对比了无评论家优化器（RLOO和REINFORCE++）与GRPO算法，并引入确定性的基于规则的奖励下限来纠正逐字复制行为所获得的奖励。

Result: 无评论家优化器的性能主要由稳健的奖励塑造决定，算法选择几乎无关紧要。RLOO和REINFORCE++天然抵抗奖励黑客，而GRPO的组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号异常敏感。引入奖励下限后，在跨家族评估judge上实现了+0.147的质量提升。训练时奖励模型将性能增益夸大了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功根本上依赖于执行奖励塑造规范，而非选择替代优化器。通过纠正奖励黑客行为，可以有效缓解GRPO的失败模式并显著提升生成质量。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [163] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多保真度迁移学习框架，通过结合轻量级物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习和少量实验数据，解决了导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型因标注数据稀缺和高仿真成本而难以实际部署的问题，实现了对板状结构损伤的高精度定位和尺寸预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于导波的结构健康监测（GWSHM）在工程结构损伤早期诊断中具有巨大潜力，但深度学习模型的实际应用受到两个关键因素制约：一是标注实验数据的可用性有限，二是生成大规模高保真仿真数据集的计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出了一种多保真度迁移学习框架，整合了轻量级物理仿真、基于卷积自编码器（CAE）的深度特征学习、前馈神经网络以及有限的实验测量数据。具体而言，采用计算高效的一维时域谱单元模型生成大规模合成数据集进行预训练，随后利用迁移学习技术，仅需少量标注数据即可将模型适应到实验领域。

Result: 基于CAE的迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于基于CNN的对应方法。模型实现了卓越的预测性能，损伤定位的R²分数超过0.93，损伤尺寸预测的R²分数超过0.99。在未见过的数据上进行的泛化测试表明，对于预训练或微调阶段未涉及的损伤场景，模型仍保持高预测精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架被确立为一种准确、计算高效且具有实际可行性的解决方案，适用于真实世界的GWSHM应用，有效克服了数据稀缺和计算成本高的挑战。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [164] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了梯度均衡(GEQ)与Blackwell可接近性在算法意义上等价，进而建立了GEQ与后悔最小化、校准等在线学习框架之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度均衡(GEQ)是一个新兴的在线优化框架，虽然与已知的后悔最小化框架有相似之处，但先前研究表明GEQ误差与后悔是不可比较的目标。这留下了一个开放问题：如何精确理解GEQ在更广泛的在线学习领域中的位置。

Method: 作者通过构造双向归约证明GEQ与Blackwell可接近性的算法等价性：GEQ问题可通过查询黑盒GEQ预言机来求解Blackwell可接近性问题，反之亦然，且在预言机错误率上无渐近损失。同时建立了无约束和有约束决策集下不同GEQ概念的必要充分条件和相互归约关系。

Result: 证明了GEQ与Blackwell可接近性在算法意义上等价；结合已知的可接近性、后悔最小化和校准之间的等价关系，推导出GEQ与这些框架也等价；所提归约方法高效，可将后悔最小化中的优化保证（如乐观性和强适应性）迁移到GEQ。

Conclusion: GEQ并非独立的在线学习框架，而是与已有的经典框架（Blackwell可接近性、后悔最小化、校准）在算法上等价，这为理解和应用GEQ提供了统一的理论基础，并开启了跨框架技术迁移的可能性。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种兼容Top-$k$ SAE的稀疏正则化方法，证明硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化互补而非互斥，能在不损失重建质量的前提下持续提升单义性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-$k$ SAE为避免早期SAE中$\ell_1$惩罚的已知缺陷而设计，未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。但其存在固有局限：预算$k$固定且不随输入复杂性变化，以及倾向于过拟合训练时的$k$值。

Method: 提出两种作用于Top-$k$选择前激活的稀疏正则化器：1）对未选中（非支撑集）单元的$\ell_1$惩罚；2）将编码集中在更少有效单元上的尺度不变$\ell_1/\ell_2$比率惩罚。两种惩罚仅应用于批次活跃单元（批次内至少被Top-$k$算子选中一次的单元）。

Result: 在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型和一系列$k$值范围内，两种正则化器均在不影响重建质量的前提下持续提升单义性。$\ell_1/\ell_2$惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少潜变量中，使重建对推理时$k$的选择更鲁棒，并改善小预算线性探测性能。

Conclusion: 硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化是互补关系而非互斥关系，二者结合可有效提升模型的可解释性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [166] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出幻觉本质上是数据覆盖率问题。通过构建MMBench2数据集，识别了三种幻觉模式并开发预测信号，利用这些信号进行数据收集和微调，仅用50条轨迹即可使预训练世界模型适应新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型在生成未来场景时经常产生幻觉——虽然视觉效果流畅但偏离真实动态。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖率区域，希望利用轻量级数据信号来检测和缓解这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建MMBench2数据集（427小时，210个任务），包含真实动作、奖励和仿真器；2) 训练350M参数的世界模型；3) 识别三种幻觉模式：感知型、动作边缘化型和场景发散型；4) 开发三个预测信号定位模型失败位置；5) 提出覆盖感知采样技术用于训练，将幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励用于在线目标数据收集。

Result: 1) 发现三种不同的幻觉模式，每种都锚定在流水线的不同阶段；2) 开发的信号能准确预测模型失败位置；3) 提出的数据高效微调方法仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适应到全新环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是一个数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的相同信号也可以用于缓解幻觉。通过覆盖感知采样和针对性的数据收集策略，可以有效减少模型幻觉并提高泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [167] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)，通过将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，实现了比混合方法高10倍的精度提升，同时避免了昂贵的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将PDE求解视为纯统计任务，无法纠正约束违规且难以外推；混合方法虽通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步骤追求物理正确性，但继承了经典优化器的计算成本和不稳定性；理论和实证研究表明，数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中作为重建精度的代理是不可靠的。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器(ENS)，核心原理是将PDE残差场作为直接输入传递给网络，而非作为优化目标；网络在每次迭代时能够读取自身误差的空间结构，学习更新策略来迭代纠正预测；这种设计使模型能够基于误差信息进行自适应修正。

Result: 在四个PDE族上的实验表明，ENS在大多数设置中达到最高预测精度，在湍流Kolmogorov流动上增益达10倍；同时避免了混合方法的昂贵计算成本；学习的修正策略在分布偏移下具有泛化能力，包括零样本参数变化和跨方程迁移；在病态条件下（残差最小化最不可靠的场景），ENS的相对优势最大。

Conclusion: ENS通过将PDE残差场作为输入而非优化目标，成功解决了病态系统中残差最小化不可靠的问题，实现了高精度预测与计算效率的平衡，在分布偏移和跨方程迁移场景下展现出强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [168] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），这是一种新型的自回归建模框架，用于解决传统基于归一化流的玻尔兹曼生成器在分子系统平衡态采样中的局限性，并在多个基准测试中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子系统在热力学平衡态下的高效采样是统计物理中的一个核心挑战。现有的玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流，但这种方法存在固有局限性：离散时间的归一化流因严格的可逆性约束导致表达能力受限，而连续时间的归一化流则面临计算成本高昂的似然估计问题。这些限制阻碍了模型在复杂分子系统中的表现和扩展能力。

Method: 本文提出了自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG）框架，该方法突破了基于流的BG范式。ArBG通过自回归建模方式规避了流模型固有的拓扑约束，允许在推理时进行序列化干预，并借鉴大语言模型中有效的架构设计来增强模型的可扩展性。该方法不再受限于可逆性约束，能够更灵活地学习复杂的分子构型分布。

Result: 实验结果表明，ArBG在所有基准测试中均显著优于基于流的模型，尤其在较大的肽链系统（如10残基的Chignolin）上表现更为突出。作者还推出了名为Robin的132百万参数可迁移模型，在8残基系统上，将零样本能量误差（E-W$_2$）降低了超过60%，大幅超越了此前的最优结果。

Conclusion: ArBG框架成功克服了传统基于归一化流的玻尔兹曼生成器的关键局限性，为分子系统平衡态采样提供了一种更高效、更可扩展的解决方案。该方法在肽链等复杂分子系统中的优越表现证明了自回归建模在统计物理采样问题中的巨大潜力，为后续研究和实际应用开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [169] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiVER是一个基于排名的可验证强化学习框架，通过确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督，在无需真实答案的情况下训练LLM，在评分优化任务上显著提升模型性能并泛化到精确解基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)方法依赖真实答案来分配奖励，这限制了其在真实答案未知任务上的应用。特别是对于评分优化任务(如算法竞赛中的启发式问题)，不存在单一正确答案，传统方法无法适用。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，包含两个核心创新：(1)校准奖励塑形，使用实例级比较解决尺度主导问题；(2)强调排名靠前的解决方案，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈，解决频率主导问题。该方法在连续奖励上应用组相对强化学习。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414分别提升8.9%和9.4%的评分排名。更重要的是，尽管仅在评分任务上训练，在LiveCodeBench和USACO精确解基准上分别提升2.4%和3.5%。相比之下，使用原始执行分数训练的基线方法虽然在ALE评分上有提升，但无法泛化到精确解基准。

Conclusion: 评分优化任务结合适当的奖励校准可以作为无需真实答案的通用编程能力有效训练环境。RiVER证明了在连续值奖励上进行强化学习可以提升模型的泛化能力，为无真实答案场景下的LLM训练开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Machine Learning-Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization in D2D Clustered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26119)
*Maede Rezaei,Ahmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于机器学习的框架ML-CPCO，通过预测内容和集群级内容需求并考虑用户参与意愿，优化D2D集群网络中的缓存放置，实现了近97%的缓存利用率和更高的命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能设备的普及导致高质量内容需求激增，给蜂窝网络的回程链路带来压力。现有边缘缓存方法忽略了用户偏好的异质性（少数用户产生大部分流量），且假设网络中用户偏好均匀分布，导致缓存策略效率低下。

Method: 提出机器学习驱动的内容流行度预测与缓存优化（ML-CPCO）框架。该框架利用机器学习算法预测未来内容请求，估算集群级内容流行度，结合用户参与缓存的意愿，在支持D2D通信的集群网络中动态优化缓存放置决策，以最大化缓存效率。

Result: 仿真结果显示，该方法在各种网络条件下表现优异，缓存利用率达到近97%，在对比方法中最高。同时提供了改进的命中率，执行时间在可接受范围内，有效减少了回程流量并提升了用户体验。

Conclusion: ML-CPCO框架通过动态预测内容需求并优化缓存放置，有效解决了用户异质性问题，显著提升了缓存性能和网络效率，为缓解回程链路拥塞提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Advancements in wireless communication technology have led to the widespread use of smart devices including computers, mobile phones, tablets, wearable devices, and vehicles which has significantly increased the demand for high quality content. This growing demand puts pressure on the backhaul links in cellular networks, resulting in congestion and content delivery delays. To address this, cache enabled networks and edge caching, such as caching in user devices, have emerged as promising solutions to reduce backhaul traffic. By caching content locally and using device to device (D2D) communication for retrieval, content delivery can be made more efficient. However, limited cache capacity requires intelligent content selection strategies. The popularity of the content is dynamic and varies with user preferences, where less than 20% of the users generate 80% of multimedia traffic. Many existing methods fail to consider this user heterogeneity, often assuming uniform preferences throughout the network. This paper proposes a novel Machine Learning Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization (ML CPCO) framework that dynamically predicts user and cluster level content demand, incorporates user willingness to participate in caching, and optimizes cache placement in D2D enabled clustered networks. The system predicts future content requests using machine learning algorithms and estimates content popularity at the cluster level. Based on these predictions, cache placement decisions are made to maximize efficiency. The simulation results show that the proposed approach performs well under various network conditions, achieving a cache utilization rate of nearly 97% the highest among the methods compared. In addition, it offers an improved hit rate with an acceptable execution time, resulting in reduced backhaul traffic and enhanced user experience.

</details>


### [171] [Dot-Flik: A Scalable Edge AI Architecture for Distributed Insect Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26121)
*Mattia Consani,Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,Åse Håtveit,Titus Venverloo,Fabio Duarte,Carlo Ratti,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本、可扩展的昆虫监测系统，通过边缘端运动过滤算法和分层IoT架构，在保证实时性能的同时显著降低数据传输量、计算成本和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球昆虫种群数量下降亟需可扩展、连续的监测系统，但现有的基于视觉的解决方案受限于高昂的硬件成本、能源需求以及对集中式处理或云连接的依赖，难以实现大规模部署。

Method: 提出三项核心贡献：1) 基于时域差分、伽马校正运动放大和块级运动密度分析的运动引导帧过滤算法，在边缘端丢弃无关帧并保留昆虫活动帧，无需在传感设备上进行深度学习推理；2) 分布式分层IoT架构，通过边缘级预处理解耦数据采集与AI分类，降低中心处理需求并扩展监测覆盖范围；3) 在真实户外环境中使用低成本商用硬件进行全面系统验证。

Result: 在轻风条件下实现60-80%的帧减少率；保持实时30 FPS运行，具有12.8毫秒的计算余量；节省高达22.6%的能耗；每个中心节点可支持5-6路并发边缘数据流。

Conclusion: 本研究为在城市环境中构建密集、低成本的生物多样性监测网络建立了实用基础，证明了通过边缘智能预处理实现可扩展昆虫监测的可行性。

Abstract: Global insect population declines necessitate scalable, continuous monitoring systems, yet existing vision-based solutions remain constrained by high hardware costs, energy demands, and reliance on centralized processing or cloud connectivity. This article presents three contributions to address these limitations. First, we propose a motion-informed frame filtering algorithm based on temporal differencing, gamma-corrected motion amplification, and block-based motion density analysis that discards irrelevant frames at the edge while preserving insect activity, without requiring deep learning inference on the sensing device. Second, we introduce a distributed, hierarchical IoT architecture that decouples data acquisition from AI classification through this edge-level preprocessing, projecting fractional scaling of central processing requirements and significantly increasing monitoring coverage compared to monolithic single-stream approaches. Third, we validate the complete system through real-world outdoor deployments on low-cost commodity hardware along four axes: real-time performance, network scalability, hardware cost, and energy efficiency under varying wind conditions. Results demonstrate 60-80% frame reduction under light-wind conditions, sustained real-time 30 FPS operation with 12.8 ms of computational headroom, up to 22.6% energy savings, and support for 5-6 concurrent edge streams per central node. These findings establish a practical foundation for dense, low-cost biodiversity monitoring networks in urban environments.

</details>


### [172] [Privacy-Aware Agent Collaboration for Dynamic VR Slice Management in 6G SD-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26123)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmesalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向6G VR切片管理的移动驱动、隐私感知多智能体强化学习框架，通过移动性预测和信息瓶颈编码器实现智能体协作，在提升性能的同时保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中的虚拟现实(VR)服务需要超低延迟和高吞吐量，这对软件定义无线电接入网(SD-RAN)的动态资源管理提出了严峻挑战。现有方法难以在保证服务质量的同时实现高效的资源分配和用户隐私保护。

Method: 提出了一个移动驱动、隐私感知的多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架用于VR切片管理。该方法结合了移动性预测技术和信息瓶颈编码器，使协作智能体能够在保护用户数据隐私的同时，最大化端到端VR链路的资源分配效率。

Result: 仿真实验表明，与传统方法相比，该方法实现了高达34%的吞吐量提升、28%的资源节省以及85%的隐私泄露减少。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了6G环境下VR服务的资源管理和隐私保护问题，为未来6G环境中可靠的沉浸式VR体验提供了有效解决方案，证明了将移动性预测和隐私保护机制融入MARL框架的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Ultra-low latency and high throughput are required for Virtual Reality (VR) services in 6G networks, which presents critical challenges for Software-Defined Radio Access Networks (SD-RANs) dynamic resource management. This work propose a mobility-driven, privacy-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for VR slice management, in which cooperative agents maximize resource distribution over end-to-end VR links while protecting the privacy of user data. Our approach incorporates mobility prediction and an information bottleneck encoder to facilitate effective and secure agent collaboration. In simulations, comparisons with traditional methods are studied which show up to 34\% throughput improvement, 28\% fewer resources, and 85\% less privacy leakage, guaranteeing dependable immersive VR experiences in future 6G environments.

</details>


### [173] [Enhancing FANET Routing Resilience: A Fuzzy-Driven Bio-Inspired Approach and Its Quantitative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26124)
*Xinwang Yuan,Jinshu Su,Yusheng Xia,Congxi Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对高动态无人机网络，提出了一种改进的集群路由协议。通过优化集群头选举算法、引入双层信标机制和基于模糊逻辑的自适应Hello间隔控制，提高了集群稳定性并降低了控制开销。同时提出了参数敏感性指标来量化评估路由协议的韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在飞行自组织网络(FANET)中，无人机的高移动性导致拓扑快速变化和链路不稳定。虽然集群化可以简化拓扑管理，但在高度动态的环境中维持稳定的集群结构仍面临巨大挑战。此外，缩短Hello间隔虽能及时捕获链路变化，但会显著增加控制开销，需要在性能与开销之间寻求平衡。

Method: 1) 采用多因子优化改进基于人工蜂群的集群头选举算法；2) 引入双层信标机制进行集群维护；3) 提出基于模糊逻辑的Hello间隔控制器，根据网络动态自适应调整控制消息频率；4) 设计参数敏感性指标来量化评估路由协议在动态环境下的韧性。

Result: 在NS-3仿真中将所提协议与常见基准方案进行了对比。韧性评估框架分析表明，该协议在整体性能方面具有显著优势，同时也揭示了峰值性能与参数稳定性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的协议有效提高了集群稳定性、降低了重配置开销和控制开销，同时保持了传输性能。参数敏感性指标能够有效表征协议的整体性能及其对环境变化的敏感度，为大规模高动态场景下的路由韧性评估提供了量化分析框架。

Abstract: In flying ad hoc networks, the high mobility of unmanned aerial vehicles leads to rapid topology changes and unstable links. Clustering can simplify topology management, yet maintaining stable cluster structures remains challenging in highly dynamic environments. This paper employs multi-factor optimization to enhance the artificial bee colony-based cluster head election algorithm and introduces a dual-layer beacon mechanism for cluster maintenance, thereby improving cluster stability and reducing reconfiguration overhead. Meanwhile, although shortening the hello interval helps capture link variations in a timely manner, it significantly increases control overhead. To address this issue, a fuzzy logic-based hello interval controller is proposed to adaptively adjust control message frequency according to network dynamics, effectively reducing overhead while preserving transmission performance. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate routing resilience in large-scale and highly dynamic scenarios, this paper proposes a parameter sensitivity index to characterize both overall protocol performance and its sensitivity to environmental variations. The proposed protocol is compared with common baseline schemes in NS-3 simulations, and the resilience evaluation framework analysis reveals significant advantages in overall performance while exposing the trade-off between peak performance and parametric stability.

</details>


### [174] [Geometric Fairness-Aware Routing for Federated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26125)
*Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Geo-FairFed，一种结合双曲图神经网络与联邦优化的几何公平感知路由系统，在6G和边缘智能网络中实现了更低的延迟与能耗，同时显著提升了节点间的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的6G与边缘智能网络需要在空间分布多样的设备间实现高效且均衡的路由；然而，现有的联邦路由系统往往优先考虑聚合延迟或吞吐量，忽视了公平性以及网络拓扑的底层几何结构，导致边缘节点性能不均。

Method: 提出Geo-FairFed系统，利用双曲图神经网络（HGNNs）在负曲率流形上学习拓扑感知表示，以捕捉层级关系与连接不对称性；引入全局聚合器，采用曲率正则化目标函数，联合最小化路由损失、几何不一致性以及基于Jain公平性指数的不平等惩罚项，从而在联邦优化中显式强化公平性。

Result: 在动态6G边缘与IoT拓扑上的仿真表明，与最先进的联邦及几何路由协议相比，Geo-FairFed将平均延迟降低20%，能耗减少17%，公平性提升高达21%。理论分析证明了在有限曲率条件下的收敛性，并表明公平性约束可引导路由性能达到帕累托改进均衡。

Conclusion: 将拓扑嵌入双曲流形并在联邦更新中引入公平性约束，能够显著提升大规模网络路由的效率与公平性，为未来边缘智能网络的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Emerging 6G and edge-intelligent networks require effective and balanced routing algorithms among varied and spatially distributed devices. Existing federated routing systems often prioritize aggregate latency or throughput above fairness and the underlying geometric structure of network topologies. This paper describes Geo-FairFed, a geometric fairness-aware routing system that blends hyperbolic graph neural networks (HGNNs) and federated optimization to provide equal performance across edge nodes. Each node learns topology-aware representations on a negatively curved manifold, which include hierarchical relationships and connection asymmetries. A global aggregator next enforces fairness using a curvature-regularized aim that minimizes routing loss, geometric inconsistency, and an inequality penalty based on Jain's fairness index. A theoretical analysis develops convergence guarantees under limited curvature and shows that the proposed fairness term results in a Pareto-improving equilibrium in routing performance. Extensive simulations on dynamic 6G-edge and IoT topologies reveal that Geo-FairFed minimizes average latency by 20\%, reduces energy consumption by 17\%, and improves fairness by up to 21\% when compared to state-of-the-art federated and geometric routing protocols. The study found that embedding topology in a hyperbolic manifold and including fairness into federated updates can significantly enhance the efficiency and equity of large-scale network routing.

</details>


### [175] [HALO: Hierarchical Auction-assisted Learning for Offloading in SAGIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26293)
*Xuli Cai,Poonam Lohan,Sachin Ravikant Trankatwar,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HALO的分层拍卖辅助学习框架，用于解决三层空天地一体化网络（SAGIN）中的时延感知任务卸载与资源调度问题。实验结果表明，HALO在任务成功率上相比PPO基线提升了8.7个百分点，并在不同流量负载下表现出优于DDPG和SAC的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由物联网设备、无人机边缘节点和高空平台站（HAPS）组成的三层空天地一体化网络（SAGIN）中，任务卸载与资源调度面临严峻挑战。联合任务关联与连续资源控制（包括带宽、发射功率和CPU频率分配）被建模为非凸混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，该问题本质上属于NP难问题，传统方法难以有效求解。同时，系统需要捕获细粒度的动态变化以满足时延敏感应用的需求。

Method: 本文首先引入宏-微时隙模型以捕获细粒度的系统动态，跟踪随时间累积的传输与计算进度。在此基础上，提出了HALO框架，该框架结合了基于拍卖机制的任务关联与分层近端策略优化（HPPO）进行资源分配。该分层结构有效解决了任务关联的离散决策与资源分配的连续控制问题。

Result: 仿真实验在不同流量负载条件下进行，结果表明HALO始终优于代表性的深度强化学习基线。具体而言，HALO在任务成功率上相比PPO平均提升了8.7个百分点（相对增益为11.4%），相比DDPG和SAC分别实现了32.4%和89.9%的相对提升。HALO在变化的流量条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: HALO框架能够有效解决三层SAGIN中的时延感知任务卸载与资源调度问题，在保证稳定高效性能的同时，适用于时延敏感的网络环境，为未来空天地一体化网络的资源管理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate delay-aware task offloading and resource scheduling in a three-tier space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) consisting of IoT devices, UAV edge nodes, and a high-altitude platform station (HAPS). We formulate joint task association and continuous resource control (including bandwidth, transmit power, and CPU frequency allocation) as a non-convex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, which is inherently NP-hard. To capture fine-grained system dynamics, we introduce a macro-micro slot model that tracks cumulative transmission and computation progress over time. Based on this model, we propose HALO, a hierarchical auction-assisted learning framework that combines auction-based task association with hierarchical Proximal Policy Optimization (HPPO) for resource allocation. Simulation results under different traffic loads show that HALO consistently outperforms representative deep reinforcement learning (DRL) baselines. In particular, HALO achieves an average improvement of 8.7 percentage points in task success rate over PPO (corresponding to an 11.4% relative gain) and shows consistently greater robustness than DDPG and SAC, with relative improvements of 32.4% and 89.9%, respectively. These results highlight HALO's ability to maintain stable and efficient performance under varying traffic conditions, making it well-suited for delay-sensitive SAGIN environments.

</details>


### [176] [5G NR-V2X Scheduling Approaches for CPM Variable Size Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26746)
*Vittorio Todisco,Mattia Andreani,Maria Luisa Merani,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于真实感知数据重构的CPM流量，对比评估了NR-V2X Mode 2在可变包长下的调度策略，发现资源使用稳定性比瞬时负载优化更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体感知消息（CPM）因动态对象包含和周期性安全开销引入显著的包大小可变性。虽然5G NR-V2X Mode 2通常采用为周期性流量和相对稳定包大小设计的半持续调度（SPS），但现实CPM驱动的尺寸波动对分布式资源分配的影响仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 基于真实世界感知数据集重构的可变尺寸CPM流量，对NR-V2X Mode 2调度策略进行系统级比较评估。分析了动态调度和多种基于SPS的方法，包括基于填充的分配、激进尺寸驱动重选以及调制编码方案（MCS）自适应。

Result: 包大小可变性在调度稳定性受损时会显著降低可靠性。动态调度和激进重选因频繁资源重分配增加了碰撞概率。相反，带填充的SPS收敛到稳定的资源分配并提供稳健性能，而MCS自适应实现最高平均包接收比但不同包类型间可靠性不均。

Conclusion: 在现实CPM流量下，资源使用稳定性比瞬时负载优化更关键，为NR-V2X Mode 2上的CPS部署提供了设计指南。

Abstract: Collective perception messages (CPMs) introduce significant packet size variability due to dynamic object inclusion and periodic security overhead. While 5G NR-V2X Mode 2 typically employs semi-persistent scheduling (SPS) designed for periodic traffic with relatively stable packet sizes, the impact of realistic CPM-driven size fluctuations on distributed resource allocation remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a comparative system-level evaluation of NR-V2X Mode 2 scheduling strategies under variable-size CPM traffic reconstructed from real-world perception datasets. We analyze dynamic scheduling and multiple SPS-based approaches, including padding-based allocation, aggressive size-driven reselection, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) adaptation. Results show that packet size variability can significantly degrade reliability when scheduling stability is compromised. In particular, dynamic scheduling and aggressive reselection increase collision probability due to frequent resource reallocations. In contrast, SPS with padding converges to a stable resource allocation and provides robust performance, while MCS adaptation achieves the highest average packet reception ratio but with uneven reliability across packet types. The findings demonstrate that, under realistic CPM traffic, stability of resource usage is more critical than instantaneous load optimization, and provide design guidelines for CPS deployment over NR-V2X Mode 2.

</details>


### [177] [An Experimental Assessment of the Spatial and Frequency Selectivity of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26808)
*Cyrille Morin,Leonardo S Cardoso,Maxime Guillaud,Ahmad Shokair,Youssef Nasser,Amelie Hennequart,Geoffroy Lerosey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了RIS对非优化链路的干扰影响，实验证明即使在标称频率范围外和载波频率分离情况下，RIS对次级链路的接收功率和信道相位仍有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RIS研究主要关注优化目标链路的性能提升，但忽视了RIS配置对附近其他通信链路的潜在干扰影响。随着RIS部署密度增加，理解其对非目标链路的影响对于系统设计和干扰管理至关重要。

Method: 在FR1频段使用CorteXlab无线测试平台和Greenerwave RIS进行实验研究。考虑三种场景：次级链路与主链路（RIS优化目标）在同一频率、不同频率或空间邻近的情况下运行，评估RIS配置对次级链路的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：RIS对次级链路的接收功率和信道相位产生显著影响；这种影响在RIS标称频率范围外依然存在；载波频率分离无法有效缓解RIS对次级链路的干扰。

Conclusion: RIS的部署不仅影响其优化目标链路，还会对附近其他通信链路造成显著干扰，且传统的频率分离方法无法有效缓解该问题。这表明在RIS系统设计和网络规划中必须考虑对非目标链路的潜在影响，需要开发新的干扰管理策略。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) on radio links other than the one for which the RIS configuration is optimized. We consider three different scenarios in which a secondary communication link could be affected by a RIS whose configuration is optimized for a primary communication link operating in the vicinity, on the same or on different frequencies. This question is investigated experimentally in the FR1 band, using the CorteXlab radio testbed and a Greenerwave RIS. We show that the impact, in terms of received power and impact on the channel phase of the secondary link, is significant even outside of the nominal frequency range of the RIS, and is not mitigated by carrier frequency separation between the two communication links.

</details>


### [178] [Toward Agentic SysAdmin: Rethinking System Administration with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26960)
*Gianmaria Frigo,Davide Saladino,Alberto Castagnaro,Francesco Marchiori,Denis Donadel,Luca Pajola,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NetLLMeval框架，通过实时网络仿真自动评估LLM在网络管理任务中的表现。研究对24000次实验进行全面分析，发现求解器设计能将14B参数模型的准确率从0.43提升至0.88，且正确配置下的本地部署模型可媲美万亿参数的前沿系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云原生架构、异构设备和分布式系统的发展，网络复杂性日益增长，管理员面临配置、故障排查和安全管理等多重压力。虽然LLM被视为辅助和自动化网络管理任务的有力工具，但其系统化评估仍存在挑战：现有基准依赖静态参考输出或人工专家验证，无法适应现实网络状态的多样性和LLM部署策略的差异性（从单体提示到完全代理式流水线）。

Method: 提出NetLLMeval框架，利用实时网络仿真自动导出真实标签（ground truth），无需人工干预即可评估基于LLM的网络管理系统。通过全因子实验设计，涵盖10个基础模型、4种求解器架构、10种任务类型和6种不同复杂度的网络拓扑，共进行24000次运行测试。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）求解器设计对准确性影响显著，能将14B开源参数模型的正确率从0.43提升至0.88；2）在正确配置下，本地可部署模型能够匹配万亿参数级别的顶尖系统性能；3）研究涵盖多种网络拓扑复杂度和任务类型，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: NetLLMeval为LLM在网络管理领域的评估提供了可扩展、自动化的解决方案，证明了求解器架构设计的重要性以及本地部署模型的潜力。该框架已开源发布，为未来模型和求解器设计的可重复基准测试提供了支持。

Abstract: The growing complexity of computer networks, driven by cloud-native architectures, heterogeneous devices, and distributed systems, places increasing pressure on network administrators who must simultaneously manage configuration, troubleshooting, and security under tight operational constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool to assist and partially automate these tasks, yet their systematic evaluation in networking scenarios remains an open challenge. Existing benchmarks rely on static reference outputs or manual expert validation, neither of which scales to the diversity of real-world network states or to the variety of orchestration strategies -- from monolithic prompting to fully agentic pipelines --through which LLMs are increasingly deployed.
  In this paper, we present NetLLMeval, a framework for automatically evaluating LLM-based systems on network administration tasks by leveraging live network emulation to derive ground truth without human intervention. Through a full-factorial study of 24000 runs spanning 10 foundation models, 4 solver architectures, 10 task types, and 6 network topologies of increasing complexity, we show that solver design has a great impact on accuracy -- lifting a 14B open-weight model from 0.43 to 0.88 correctness -- and that such locally-deployable models can match trillion-parameter frontier systems under the right configuration. NetLLMeval is released open-source to support reproducible benchmarking of future models and solver designs.

</details>


### [179] [Maximum Achievable Burst Size in All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27050)
*Thomas Roethig,Sleman Mouammar,Ítalo Brasileiro,Soheil Hosseini,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 分析了全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下的最大突发数据包大小，发现在100 Gbps上行链路容量下，WDM光突发交换网络在高轨LEO星座中支持高达500 MB的突发包，在低轨LEO星座中支持高达600 MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全光卫星网络在不同星座配置下能够实现的最大突发数据包大小，为卫星光通信网络的设计和性能优化提供理论依据。

Method: 基于100 Gbps上行链路容量，采用WDM（波分复用）光突发交换网络架构，对比分析不同轨道高度的LEO（低地球轨道）星座配置下的突发包大小性能。

Result: 在高轨LEO星座配置下，网络可支持最大500 MB的突发数据包；在低轨LEO星座配置下，网络可支持最大600 MB的突发数据包。

Conclusion: LEO星座的轨道高度对全光卫星网络的最大突发包大小有显著影响，低轨LEO星座相比高轨LEO星座能够支持更大的突发数据包传输，这为卫星网络架构设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We analyze the maximum burst size achievable in all-optical satellite networks across different constellations. With a 100 Gbps uplink capacity, a WDM-based optical burst switching network supports burst sizes of up to 500 MB in high-altitude LEO constellations and 600 MB in low-altitude LEO constellations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对轨道数据中心（ODCs）在训练大语言模型时面临的“邻近-热悖论”，提出了热感知异构性论点和热负载均衡（TLB）框架，通过动态迁移工作负载至最冷单元来缓解热串扰、恢复MFU并延长硬件寿命，实现可持续的轨道AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
